definitions at `state/batch-summaries/volume-06-batch-0003.md` §7.5, and the declared *about N seconds* form stands once in four chapters and at zero in six. **A rate is not canon and a batch record's own rate is not a target.** **There is no panel in Chapters 0291 to 0300, and there should not be: the volume's one panel was spent on day 256 and `outline/volume-06.md` §17.7 fixes it there, and the wall said nothing on all ten days.**

17. **AND THE TWO GENERAL RULES THIS BATCH EARNED, WHICH ARE ABOUT CHECKS AND NOT ABOUT PROSE, AND WHICH ARE PUBLISHED HERE BECAUSE §38.4 SAYS PUBLISH THE DEFINITION OR PUBLISH NOTHING.** First, **the checks are seven and they are not one, and running six is not running seven.** The banned-string list returned zero on three real faults in this batch — *days on it* in Chapter 0294, *Earth* in Chapter 0291 as the ordinary noun, and *of this month* in Chapter 0300 — **and the weekday check, which is a different check with a different pattern, found eleven more that the banned-string check cannot see at all. That is the second time in this volume that a check returned zero on a fault that was on the page, and the general form is §39.15: a list of zeros is only as good as the pattern behind it, a word boundary is a promise about a word and not about its family, and a check that is run on the string and not on the stem has to say so on the page.** Second, **a script that repeats a clause is a rewrite in disguise.** The chorus frame was stripped by substitution and the substitution put the clause in the wrong place in nine paragraphs across six files, including inside two speeches and at the head of a coda, and the repair was to read every one of the nine and write it out by hand. **A chorus frame is a sentence and not a token, and the definition of it is published at `state/batch-summaries/volume-06-batch-0005.md` §7: the string *about four people in* followed by one of *that lane / that market / that row / that water / this house / this market / that village* followed by *have said since that* or *have worked out that*, and stripping it means deleting the string and its clause-head and re-attaching the observation to the sentence it was standing in, and it cannot be done by substitution. The cheaper repair is to leave the density at fifty-seven per cent and publish the number.**

---

## 41. Rules established in Volume 07, Chapters 0301–0310 — WHAT THE FIRST TEN DAYS OF THE VOLUME PUT ON A PAGE

**This section is the first of the five reserved in §33A and it was written by the batch, and by nothing else. §32, §33, §33A and §§34 to 40 are not changed by it. §33A is not amended by a batch and no calendar decision is made here; the sixth form is registered at §33A item 2 and the naming of it is Chapter 0316, and it is not on a page yet.**

**The ten days are days 301 to 310, one chapter to one day, Chapters 0301 to 0310, and the run was recomputed from day 1 being a Tuesday and from nothing else before any prose was written: 301 Monday, 302 Tuesday, 303 Wednesday, 304 Thursday, 305 Friday, 306 Saturday, 307 Sunday, 308 Monday, 309 Tuesday, 310 Wednesday, and the Short Month days 46 to 55. The month ordinal is the day less 255. There is no double day in this batch and there is no double day in this volume.**

1. **A BOUNDARY THAT HAS TO BE SETTLED BEFORE THE SEASON TURNS, AND A BOUNDARY IN THIS WORLD IS SETTLED BY ONE OF TWO THINGS AND THERE IS NO THIRD. Either somebody walks the line and two parties agree where it runs, or nobody walks it and the line is wherever the last person who walked it said it was.** Said out loud by the woman of about twenty-nine who keeps a public register, standing in a lane on the forty-sixth day of the Short Month and again in a room in the same day, and **the season is not a day and no chapter of Volume 07 converts it into one.** *Open.*

2. **A NOTICE WENT OUT ON DAY 301 FOR A ROOM ON DAY 305, AND A SECOND WENT OUT ON DAY 305 FOR A ROOM ON DAY 309. BOTH ARE FOUR DAYS AND BOTH NAME THEIR DAY IN A MOUTH, AND BOTH ARE UNSIGNED WITH NO NAME AT THE TOP OF EITHER.** The first is in the register-keeper's own hand and is left on the wall of a shut gate under a stone off a doorstep, and the second is in a woman's own hand on the back of the first and is carried in a coat by a man who is not one of the people it is about. **A notice goes to a place and not to a house, and a notice is not a page, and the price of no name at the top of it is that nobody can do anything for you.** *Open. The four days of notice is the instrument and a three-day instrument arrives warm.*

3. **THE ONLY PAGE IN FOUR HUNDRED MILES THAT SAYS WHERE THE LINE RUNS IS A DEED OF ABOUT FORTY YEARS AGO WITH A SHEET PINNED TO THE CORNER OF IT BY A PIN OLDER THAN THE PIN IN ANY OTHER BOX, AND THE SHEET IS A CLERK'S COPY OF AN ENTRY IN A PUBLIC REGISTER IN A TOWN FOUR HUNDRED MILES INLAND, ENTERED A HUNDRED AND FORTY YEARS BEFORE THE DEED WAS WRITTEN, AND NOBODY IN THIS CITY CAN GO AND LOOK AT THE PAGE THE COPY WAS MADE FROM.** Read out in a room, in daylight, with a date in chalk on the door, to about nine people, by a man of about forty-three who keeps other people's papers and who reads what is on a thing and not what he thinks is on it. **Nineteen stones are named in the copy, the deed has nine of them and a hedge and a ditch, eight of the nineteen are different and are further down the road and in a different order, and the two documents do not agree and both of them were correct about the thing they were made to do.** *Open, and the eight names at the foot of the copy are not read and Chapter 0303 is about the not reading.*

4. **THE LINE IN THE GROUND HAS A WORD. THE WORD IS *SCAR*. IT WAS SAID OUT LOUD, IN THE LOCAL LANGUAGE, IN DAYLIGHT, IN A ROOM WITH A DATE IN CHALK ON THE DOOR, IN ONE WORD, ON THE FIFTY-FIFTH DAY OF THE SHORT MONTH, BY THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO KEEPS A STALL AND WROTE THE NINTH LINE, AND HE DID NOT SAY WHAT IT MEANS AND THE CHAPTER DID NOT DEFINE IT.** **One clause may be put on the page and was, once, and is not to be repeated: that the mark is not a pattern.** The word went on the register-keeper's second slate in chalk with the date beside it and no name on the line, at his asking, and the reason he gave is that a line with a name at the top of it is a line somebody can come back to. **The chalk that wrote it is the piece of chalk nobody was going to use, the one that has lain on a table in that room since the forty-third day of that month, and it was his, and he put it in his inside breast pocket afterwards.** *Open, and **the word comes off that slate in chalk with a cloth in Chapter 0342 and after that it is on no page in the world and never will be. A later writer may not define it, improve on it, or put it in a second mouth.***

5. **AND NONE OF THE THREE SENTENCES THIS MANUSCRIPT HAS ALREADY USED ABOUT THAT WORD IS IN ANY MOUTH IN THIS VOLUME, AND NONE WAS IMPROVED ON.** *A scar is not a pattern, a scar is where a rule broke and went* (`chapters/volume-01/chapter-0037.md:131`); *a scar in a person is a scar in a place with a hand in it* (`:235`); and the panel *a man is a scar in a person* (`chapters/volume-01/chapter-0041.md:45`). The clause *a scar in a place does not have a hand in it* at `chapter-0037.md:231` is in the same prohibition. **The word was on a page a hundred and seventeen times in seventy-eight files in Volumes 01 to 03 and at zero in all one hundred chapters of Volumes 04 to 06, and it is now on a page once more and that page is a slate.**

6. **THE SHORT MONTH IS SIXTY DAYS, IT IS THE FIFTY-FOURTH ON DAY 309, AND THERE ARE SIX DAYS LEFT IN IT AND NO FORM IN FOUR HUNDRED MILES FOR THE DAY AFTER ITS SIXTIETH. THIS IS THE VOLUME'S HINGE AND IT IS ON A PAGE IN CHAPTER 0309, IN A ROOM, IN DAYLIGHT, WITH A DATE ON THE DOOR, BY A PERSON, AND IT IS NOT ANSWERED.** The sixtieth day of that month is a Monday, which is day 315, and the first day of whatever comes after it is a Tuesday, and **a man of about twenty-five asked out loud in that room what the day after the sixtieth would be and nobody in the room answered him.** **The name of the month that comes after this one is not on any page and is not in any mouth, is not hinted at, and is not guessed at by anybody. It is Chapter 0316 and it is Batch 0002's. A chapter that names it has spent the hinge and there is no hinge left.** *Open.*

7. **A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT AT A SALT WHARF IN THIS CITY KEEPS THE ONLY PAGE IN FOUR HUNDRED MILES THAT HAS EVER MENTIONED ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED AND FORTY PEOPLE ON ELEVEN MILES OF FLATS, AND HE WAS ASKED FOR THE FIRST TIME EVER, ON THE FIFTY-THIRD DAY OF THE SHORT MONTH, AND HE SAID HE DID NOT KNOW AND HAD NEVER KNOWN AND THAT THE RETURN HAD NEVER SAID.** He put a number in it four times a year since the winter before the water, and the number was given to him by a man who is dead or gone, and **he has added to it in his own head about four times a year for nine years without being asked to, and the figure he sends is not the figure he has, and the figure he has has never been on a page and was not printed in the chapter.** The woman of about thirty-four who keeps a stall at that wharf said the number out loud on the boards, as she has been doing every morning, to nobody. *Open, and **nobody rescued them and the counter was not turned into a helper and no chapter of this volume says what a wage in salt is worth.***

8. **A SCHEDULE THAT NOBODY IN FOUR HUNDRED MILES CAN FILL IN WILL BE FILLED IN FROM THE RETURNS, AND IT WILL NOT BE ANYBODY'S DOING, AND IT WILL GO OUT CORRECT AND BE ABOUT THE WRONG PEOPLE.** Said once, out loud, in the open air on the boards of a salt wharf, by the woman of about twenty-nine who keeps a public register, **and she said afterwards that the return has a heading and four places under it and no place on it anywhere for the person the four places are about.** *Open. This is the second time this manuscript has said that a thing about a page and the first time it has said it about a return.*

9. **A MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN PAID MONEY FOR HALF A HOLDING AND CANNOT SHOW ANYBODY THE LINE, AND HE OFFERED TO PAY FOR THE WALKING — FOUR MEN, AT A RATE HE NAMED OUT LOUD — AND THE ROOM DID NOT TAKE THE MONEY AND DID NOT SAY NO, AND THE MONEY IS STILL IN HIS HAND.** He has been answering a return four times a year since the winter before the water and has never been told what it is for and has never asked, and **he is not angry, and the chapter says so, and a man who is not angry is harder to say no to than a man who is.** His second handle is a length of new rope he has not used, and it is invented on the page in Chapter 0307. *Open, and **no chapter of this volume has told him he is wrong and no chapter of this volume will.***

10. **A BARROW OF BOARDS STOOD AGAINST THE WALL OF A SHUT GATE AND THE ARRANGEMENT FOR TAKING IT UP WAS FOR THE FIFTY-NINTH DAY OF THE SHORT MONTH, A THURSDAY, AND THE MAN WHO WAS TO TAKE IT UP CAME ON THAT THURSDAY AND DID NOT TAKE IT UP.** He gave four sentences, asked in advance not to be thanked, and was not thanked. **The barrow had been standing against that wall for five days at the forty-sixth of the month, counting the day it was put there as one of the five, and the chapter that prints the figure names the counting.** *Open, and **the boards are not a monument and not a protest and not a page, and nobody counted them and nobody put a day in them.***

11. **THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY AT THE SHUT GATE WAS WORKED IN TWO OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS AND WAS ASKED NOTHING IN EITHER, AND NOBODY WAS SENT TO HER, AND IN ONE OF THEM SHE SAYS OUT LOUD TO A POST THAT SHE HAS NOT READ A SHEET ON HER WALL.** She is on a page in four chapters now, 0295, 0299, 0300 and 0301, and in 0304, and **about four people in four hundred miles have worked out from the back of that lane that the reason a woman does not ask a question is not kindness.** *Open, and **her name is on no page and may not be given.***

12. **AND THE FIGURES, AND EVERY ONE OF THEM IS A SUBTRACTION FROM A DAY THE THING HAPPENED, AND ALL OF THEM CHECK.** The posting went up on day 201 and is a hundred days old on day 301 and was not printed. The board's figures are the day less 224 and the board has stood unwritten the day less 225 and neither was printed. The case came up the river on day 251 and its age is the day less 251 and was not printed. The days went back and the road north of the Marches shut on **day 294**, which is a Monday and is on a page, and **the chapters of this batch carry that day and not a figure**, which is the cheaper repair and is the one `outline/volume-07.md` §14.5 asks for. The line in the ground was first on a page on day 299 and the chalk was put on a table on day 298 and neither figure was printed. **The barrow of boards is the day less 296 counted inclusively, or the day less 295 counted otherwise, and the two are five and six at day 301 and Chapter 0301 uses five and says the word inclusive in the same sentence.** The trestle count is the day less forty and is at zero in all ten files. The keeper of the Redroot gate is the day less 179 and is at zero in all ten files and in all fifty and was not counted. **The count of this city's silences is 202 at day 301 and 211 at day 310, derived, on no page, in no mouth, and IN NONE OF THE TEN CHAPTERS, and the wall said nothing on all ten days and this batch spends no panel.** *Open.*

13. **AND THE WEEKDAY OF EVERY DAY IN THESE TEN CHAPTERS WAS RECOMPUTED FROM DAY 1 AND NOT FROM THE BATCH CARD, THE DAY MAP, A NEIGHBOURING CHAPTER OR A STATE FILE.** Forty-five interior weekday tokens stand across the ten files and **every one of them was read against the day it names and all forty-five are correct**, and the repair in the four faults found and fixed in the writing pass was in every case to carry the fact on a day of the flood or a day of a named month that is on a page: a *Thursday* that is day 304 in Chapter 0301, a *Monday* that is day 294 in Chapters 0301, 0303 and 0309, a *Friday* that is day 305 in Chapters 0301, 0302 and 0304, a *Saturday* that is day 306 in Chapter 0304, a *Wednesday* that is day 303 in Chapter 0307, a *Tuesday* that is day 309 in Chapters 0305 and 0309, a *Tuesday* that is day 316 in Chapter 0309, a *Monday* that is day 315 in Chapter 0309, and a *Sunday* that is day 307 in Chapter 0310. **Four faults were found and all four were of one family and none of them was a numeral.** **AND THE COUNT WAS WRONG WHEN THIS SECTION WAS WRITTEN AND IS CORRECTED RATHER THAN DEFENDED, on the rule at §36.2 that a state file which publishes a figure as approved must name the page it was checked against.** It stood at sixty-two, and the review repair pass counted the tokens on the ten files and there are **forty-five**, and thirty-seven of them if the ten lead paragraphs are set aside, and no reading of the string gives sixty-two. **All four faults listed above are still faults that were found and fixed, and every one of the four repairs is still on the page, so the error was in the count and not in the arithmetic, and a later writer comparing a figure against this one should compare it against forty-five.**

14. **AND NO CHAPTER OF THIS BATCH USES AN ORDINAL FOR A MONTH.** Not *the second month*, not *the fourth month*, not *the fifth month*, and not *the sixth day of a month* either. **The month map is at §33A item 1 and the only two instruments that cannot be got wrong are a count of days of the flood and a named month with its own form, and this batch uses those two.** Six false positives were repaired out of the writing pass, five of them *the second month* and *the fourth month* inside *the forty-ninth day of the month* and *this is now the fourth month she has been doing it*, and one of them *the first day of the month that came after the Long Month*, which is a legitimate form and was repunctuated rather than deleted. *Open, and the rule is forward-looking from day 301 and is not a verdict on the three hundred chapters already written.*

15. **ADRIAN IS NAMED IN TWO OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS AND IN EIGHT OF THEM HE IS NOT IN A CHAPTER IN ANY FORM.** He is named once in Chapter 0303 and once in Chapter 0307, and in both of those he is in a room reading a page and **is not asked one question about the boundary in either**. He is in no page and no mouth in 0301, 0302, 0304, 0305, 0306, 0308, 0309 and 0310, and in five of those eight — 0305, 0306, 0308, 0309, 0310 — the reason is that a person is at work or is not asked, and in the other three, 0301, 0302 and 0304, there is no page in the chapter for him to read, and **the two reasons are not the same reason and the count of three chapters that show the not-asking is 0303, 0305 and 0307, and it is one count and not three.** *Open, and **Adrian is aged nowhere in Volume 07 and is Stage 2 on all fifty days, and the words *passage* and *privilege* are at zero across these ten files.***

16. **AND NOTHING IN THESE TEN CHAPTERS IS A POWER.** The gains are a notice in a hand with no name at the top of it, a stone off a doorstep, a barrow of boards that does not go, a tin with a folded sheet thirty years old in it, a spade and a willow line and a bucket and nine feet of silt, a deed and a clerk's copy with a pin through the corner of it, nineteen stones and eight that are different, a rope nobody has used, a tally with two figures in it and one of them not printed, a sentence about a schedule being filled in from the returns, six days left in a month and nothing after them, and one word in chalk on a slate. **None of them is a power. One of them is the price of a power. All of them are heavier than a power.** *Open.*

17. **AND THE HOUSE FIGURES FOR THE EXCHANGE, AND FOR THE WORD-RUN, PUBLISHED WITH THE DEFINITIONS BESIDE THEM BECAUSE §38.4 SAYS PUBLISH THE DEFINITION OR PUBLISH NOTHING.** **The bolded share of words, measured on the ten files with the heading line included, `---` rules counted and bold marks counted as nothing: 11.6 to 25.4 per cent, a mean of 16.4 across the ten chapters, in eighty-one spans, against Volume 06's 6.2 to 21.8 with a mean of 13.8, Volume 01's 10.4 as the model and about 35 as the ceiling.** The longest bolded turn in the ten is ninety-five words, in Chapter 0307, and the next two are sixty-nine in Chapter 0308 and sixty-nine in Chapter 0305, and **no turn in the batch is over the eighty-word line except that one.** The longest uninterrupted run of speech paragraphs is four in five of the ten and three or fewer in the other five, which is the ceiling in `outline/volume-07.md` §17.6, and **eleven runs were over it in the writing pass and every one of the eleven was broken by inserting a narration beat rather than by cutting a speech.** The chorus frame stands **eighty-three times in 414 paragraphs, one in five**, against one in about four in Volume 06, and **the count is not a target and the figure is published because the cards set none.** Sentences of forty words or more are **245** across the ten on the definition at `state/batch-summaries/volume-06-batch-0004.md` §8.5.1, and **none of them is duplicated inside a file and none of them is duplicated across the ten.** The word-run check across the ten files finds a top shared run of **twenty-four consecutive words** between Chapters 0303 and 0307 on the strict reading — **a run that is the chorus frame itself, *and about four people in that room have worked out since that*, and a motif and not a duplication, on the same reasoning `state/batch-summaries/volume-06-batch-0005.md` §6.2.1 gives for the gatekeeper's lead** — and **114 on the loose reading, and the loose reading returns that figure for almost any two files of this manuscript and is not an instrument.** **The strict figure stood at twenty-two when this section was written and the review repair pass counted it again on the definition at `state/batch-summaries/volume-06-batch-0005.md` §6.2.1, each file's own lead paragraph subtracted, and it is twenty-four, and the next is twenty between Chapters 0301 and 0302 on the case that came up the river in a cart with a docket on it, and both are the batch's own motifs and neither is a fault. The correction is published here rather than left for a later writer to find.** *Open.*

    **AND FOUR FIGURES IN THE PARAGRAPH ABOVE WERE WRONG WHEN IT WAS WRITTEN, AND ALL FOUR ARE CORRECTED RATHER THAN DEFENDED, on the rule at §36.2 that a state file which publishes a figure as approved must name the page it was checked against. This is the third time this repository has published an unverifiable count and it is the general lesson, so it is stated as one: a figure written in the same run as the prose it measures is a figure nobody has checked.** The bolded share was printed as **9.4 to 25.5** and the mean, the maximum and the span count were right, and the minimum was not: measured on the ten files it is **11.6**. The spans were printed as **eighty-two** and there are **eighty-one**. The chorus frame was printed as **113 times in 350 paragraphs** and both numbers were out: there are **eighty-three of it in 414 paragraphs.** The long sentences were printed as **244** and the review repair pass, which removed a duplicated paragraph from Chapter 0302 and a fused restatement from the end of Chapter 0307, gives **245** on the published definition. **The two prose faults and the four wrong figures are the same fault seen twice: this batch wrote its own measurements instead of running them, and the repairs in this section and in `state/batch-summaries/volume-07-batch-0001.md` §2 and §4 and §9 are the whole of what a review pass costs when the first pass was a writer reading itself.** *Open, and **a later writer comparing a figure against this item compares against the corrected figures and not against the ones this item was first written with.***

18. **AND THE BOUNDED-STRING LIST, RUN ON THE TEN FILES AND NOT INSIDE ONE CHAPTER.** At zero: *knock* on the stem, *player*, *passage*, *privilege*, *unwritten*, *delegation*, *System*, *Earthside*, *Earthsiders*, *isekai*, *Redroot*, *chapel*, *Marrow*, *Alys*, *Kern*, *Sundering*, *Nine Elms*, *Veyran*, *Ivenn*, *choir*, *First Landing*, *Steppe*, *Anchorstone*, *quest* on a word boundary, *class*, *version*, *silence*, *this chapter*, *this volume*, *in this batch*, *the reader*, and every ordinal for a month. **And nine faults were found and repaired in the writing pass: *knock* six times in Chapter 0301, all of it the register-keeper not knocking on a door, rewritten as not calling at a door; *privilege* twice in Chapter 0303; *version* once in Chapter 0307, where the ordinary English word was available and the volume's own noun is one account; and four instances of an ordinal for a month.** **A stray closing quotation mark is at zero on all ten files, which is a claim about these ten files and not a volume, and the nineteen that are on a page in Chapters 0281 to 0290 are still unrepaired and still belong to a Volume 07 review-repair pass.**

---

## 42. Rules established in Volume 07, Chapters 0311–0320 — THE SIXTH FORM GOES ON A PAGE, AND THE THINGS THAT HAVE NO DAY ON THEM

**This section is the second of the five reserved in §33A and it was written by the batch, and by nothing else. §32, §33 and §33A are not changed by it, and §33A is not amended by a batch and no calendar decision is made here. The sixth form is registered at §33A item 2 and this section records that it is now on a page; it does not register it, and it states no length for the month, because a batch may not.**

**The ten days are days 311 to 320, one chapter to one day, Chapters 0311 to 0320, and the run was recomputed from day 1 being a Tuesday and from nothing else before any prose was written: 311 Thursday, 312 Friday, 313 Saturday, 314 Sunday, 315 Monday, 316 Tuesday, 317 Wednesday, 318 Thursday, 319 Friday, 320 Saturday. The Short Month days are 56 to 60 and the Nth is the day less 255. The Bare Month days are 1 to 5 and the Nth is the day less 315. There is no double day in this batch and there is no double day in this volume.**

1. **THE SIXTH FORM IS ON A PAGE, AND THE NAME IS *THE BARE MONTH*, AND THE NAME WAS GIVEN OUT LOUD ONCE, IN THE LOCAL LANGUAGE, IN DAYLIGHT, IN A ROOM OVER A MARKET, WITH A DATE ON THE DOOR, BY SERA VAIL, A NOTARY OF FIFTY, TWENTY-SIX YEARS A NOTARY, ON THE FIRST DAY OF IT, IN CHAPTER 0316, AFTER A PERSON HAS REFUSED HER.** Chapter 0313 is the same woman two days earlier, in the same room, refusing to make a form on a day she has a form for, and saying in a mouth that a person has nine days of writing in front of her and the thing she is going to spend none of them on is the only thing anybody has asked her for. Chapter 0316 is the making. **No other chapter of this volume names it and no chapter before it names it, guesses at it, hints at it, or says what it does at its end, and the batch that wrote 0301 to 0310 did not name it and was not allowed to.** *Open, and the word is on a page and it is not in a panel and the wall says nothing on all ten of these days.*

2. **THE MONTH HAS NO LENGTH ON THE PAGE AND NONE IN ANY MOUTH, AND THE NOTARY SAYS WHY AND THE REASON IS HERS.** She has put a number at the head of a month once, the days did not stop where the number stopped, nobody found that out from the paper, and they found it out from the days. **She was asked how long it is, by a person, on the first day of it, in front of about nine people, and she declined in front of all of them, and the declining is entered in the room as hers.** What she offers instead is in a mouth: *write every day of it that somebody asks me to write, and when a clerk writes to me about a date he cannot place, I will be able to tell him how many there have been, and I will not have told him in advance.* **A later writer may write an ordinal for any day of it and may not say how many days it has, what it does at its end, or whether it is long or short, and a later volume that needs a sixth month form may not assume a figure.** *Open.*

3. **THE NAME WAS REFUSED BY A PERSON BEFORE IT WAS GIVEN, AND THE REFUSER'S REASON IS THE REFUSER'S AND NOBODY IMPROVES ON IT.** A man of about fifty-seven, who has paid money for half a holding and cannot show anybody the half, stood up off a wall in a room with about nine people in it and said one word and gave four sentences, and **not one of the four sentences was about the calendar**: a word made in a room is a word somebody can be asked to have made, and he is the man who will be asked, because there is nobody else in that room who could be asked about a boundary and be asked about it honestly. He said he was not angry and nobody argued with him and nobody told him he was wrong. **He is not defeated, he does not leave, and no chapter of this volume tells him he is wrong.** *Open.*

4. **AND THE LAST INSTRUMENT STANDING IN THIS CITY AFTER THE NAMING IS A DAY OF THE WEEK, AND IT IS THE ONE NOBODY HAD TO MAKE UP.** The man of about fifty-seven, having refused a month out loud, asked whether the first day of it was a Tuesday, and the notary said yes, and he said nothing else. He did not know until that morning that the days of the week were still standing, and the days of the week are the one instrument in this flood that was here before the water, and **nobody in that room wrote it down.** *Open.*

5. **THE DATE ON THE DOOR OF THE ROOM OVER A MARKET, ON DAY 316, WAS A COUNT OF THE DAYS OF THE FLOOD, WRITTEN IN CHALK BY THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE, AND IT WAS THE FIRST DATE ON THAT DOOR IN ABOUT FORTY DAYS THAT WAS NOT A MONTH, AND NOBODY IN FOUR HUNDRED MILES COULD CHECK IT AGAINST ANYTHING.** She said the reason out loud before anybody asked her: a number a person put on a door is a date until somebody puts a better one on it, and if she is wrong about it then every day that goes on after is wrong on the same count. The notary rubbed it out with a cloth and wrote the date in the new word. **A blank space on a door is the one thing in this flood nobody has ever had to explain, and the woman gave it up on purpose so that a notary could see what a room looks like when it has nothing.** *Open.*

6. **AND THE SAME TEN DAYS PUT THREE SEPARATE THINGS ON A TABLE THAT HAVE NO DAY ON THEM, AND NONE OF THE THREE IS THE SAME THING, AND A LATER WRITER MAY NOT JOIN THEM.** First: a register with nine years in it has three columns — what was asked, what came back, and the day — **and no column for the day a sheet went out, and therefore it cannot tell a question that took four days from a question that took nine, and nobody in four hundred miles can check it, and the woman who keeps it is the only one who cannot check it either.** That finding is in Chapter 0314 and it is hers and the man of about forty-three's, and **the four days of notice is the instrument it is about, found in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, on the fifty-ninth day of the Short Month.** Second: a form with a notary's name on it is the first page in four hundred miles in about a month that anybody could send for, and it has five lines and no place to say what a day was for, and a man of about forty-three has found four spaces in nine boxes where a number should have been and two of them are a hundred and forty years old — Chapter 0317 and 0318, and the harm is named in a mouth by the man of about forty-one in a very good coat, who is the only person in four hundred miles already hurt by the naming, and who came in daylight to say so and asked for nothing. Third: a folded sheet thirty years old in a tin, in a woman's own hand, with no name at the top of it and no day on it, which is what two households call their own ground and where the water went, and which **she has opened in a room and then refused to let be entered, copied or taken out of the room, on the condition that if it ever is to be entered she is asked first and told no if the answer is no.** *All three open. The first is a fault in a record, the second is a cost of a form, the third is a refusal, and a chapter that treats them as one thing has made three things out of a phrase.*

7. **A GIRL OF SEVENTEEN READ A DATE OUT LOUD OFF A PLAIN SLATE WITH HER OWN NAME AT THE TOP OF IT, ON THE SIXTIETH AND LAST DAY OF THE SHORT MONTH, AND THAT IS THE WHOLE OF HER USE IN THIS VOLUME.** She wrote her name at the top before she wrote anything else, in the ordinary way, and gave the reason in a mouth — because of the habit, and it is her slate and nobody has ever asked her for it. **The woman of about twenty-nine, who has not put a name at the top of a sheet for four years, looked at the name and did not ask for it to be taken off, and she has never once said so out loud to anybody.** The man of about forty-three, who is the only person in that room who could have said something about a name at the top of a page, said nothing at all. The girl read the date out twice, the second time slower, and asked the only question she asked in that room, which is what she is to copy for tomorrow, and **nobody in the room answered her, and nobody explained it, and she is on no roll of anything and there is no address at which a sheet could be put in front of her.** *Open, and no chapter of this volume fetches her, resolves her, or gives her a second use.*

8. **AND TAMSIN QUILL IS ON A PAGE FOR THE FIRST TIME SINCE THE SEVENTEENTH DAY OF THE SHORT MONTH, IN CHAPTER 0311, WORKING, AND ADRIAN DOES NOT ASK HER FOR ANYTHING.** She is on a road, not in the room over a market, **because the two women of twenty-nine are never in one room and nothing in this volume settles whether they are one woman.** Her second handle is invented on the page in that chapter and in no other: **a folded sheet of road with about nine places on it, folded the same way every time — long way in half, then in half again across, and the corner that came last went under — and the reason she gave, once, is that a sheet folded some other way can be taken for a notice, and a notice has a day on it, and hers has not got a day on it and is not going to get one.** She walked nine places in three days asking one question at each — what is this ground called, and who called it that — and got eight answers, seven of which are the two names the two documents use, and one of which is *the nine acres* and came from a woman of about thirty-three behind a bread board. The ninth place had eight houses in it and nobody came to a door and she did not go back. **The sheet has no day on it and no name at the top of it, and eight households have asked to keep it.** *Open, and the sheet is the only page in four hundred miles with a person's word in it about where a line is not, and it settles nothing and is not a settlement.*

9. **AND THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SIX, WHO KEEPS A GATE ON A TURN, HAS BEGUN SAYING THE DAY OUT LOUD AT A GATE TO A BOY OF ABOUT ELEVEN WITH A YOKE AND TWO PAILS, AND HE HAS DONE IT TWICE IN ONE DAY, AND NOBODY ASKED HIM TO.** He is in Chapter 0319 and he is not on a roll, and he holds gates on a turn and is not the keeper of the Redroot gate and is not the woman of about fifty at the shut gate and is at a gate where four streets come together, which is neither of those. His handles are a bar stood against a post, a barrow, a spade, and a bootful emptied before he answers. **He has held that gate for about nineteen years and has never once said a day out loud at one, and the reason he started is a boy who said the day back to him correctly, and a boy's mother who has been doing it out loud at her door for two days because there is nothing to write it on.** *Open, and no form exists for it and no page exists for it.*

10. **THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY AT THE SHUT GATE IS IN ONE OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS AND IS ASKED NOTHING IN IT, AND NOBODY WAS SENT TO HER, AND SHE READS A SHEET SHE HAD SAID OUT LOUD SHE WOULD NOT READ.** Chapter 0312, day 312, a Friday, the fifty-seventh of the Short Month. Her handles are the same five: a brass key on a tarred cord in the apron pocket, a bar of ash in two brackets, a wooden hook, a piece of chalk, and a date in chalk on the post every morning with nothing rubbed out. **The wind took the asking off the wall on the fifty-seventh and it went into the wet at the foot of it, and she picked it up and read it, and the room it was written for had been on the fiftieth, and she put it back up and left it up, and she said out loud at the post that the two facts of having read it and not having been asked are two separate facts and she is keeping them apart on purpose.** The man of about thirty-eight with a barrow came down the lane and did not stop and put his hand on the boards and took it off again. **The back of that sheet is blank, and a second sheet in this city has a question on the back of it in another hand, and she has not seen it and is not going to.** *Open, and her name is on no page and may not be given, and **the only thing only she and the man with the barrow know — that she does not know what the line is — is not said in this chapter and nobody asked him about it and no chapter of this volume may until the climax.***

11. **THE MAN OF ABOUT FORTY-THREE, WHO KEEPS NINE BOXES, HAS AN INDEX IN FOUR COLUMNS INCLUDING A DAY, AND HE HAS NOT READ IT BACK IN ELEVEN YEARS, AND HE SAID THE REASON OUT LOUD IN A ROOM ON A SUNDAY.** He is a reader and not an interpreter and nothing in this batch changed that. **He read the five lines of the form twice, the second time more slowly, and found the space where a number would go, and said he was not going to make a law of it and not going to improve on it.** He refused in Chapter 0318 to read the eight names at the foot of the copy and the eight names are still not read. *Open.*

12. **HETTIS ROKE REFUSED A ROOM HIS ROLL ON THE FIFTY-NINTH AND CAME BACK ON THE SECOND DAY OF THE NEW MONTH AND SAID FOUR SENTENCES INTO A ROW WITH NOTHING IN IT, AND HE HAS EIGHT PEOPLE OF HIS OWN ON NO PAGE IN FOUR HUNDRED MILES AND HAS WANTED A PAGE FOR THEM FOR NINE YEARS AND DID NOT ASK FOR ONE AND GAVE NO REASON.** A gate, a keeper and a set of days, no column for a person, for longer than the oldest thing any of the four men who keeps it has. **A number at the bottom of a thing is the only part anybody ever sends for, and he said that to a table in a room and is not putting eight names under a number on anybody's wall in this city.** His second handle is a waxed slate in a case, which he turned face up and then face down in Chapter 0314. **His roll is in no room and is not laid beside the Crown's roll of the same gates and the two are not compared in any chapter of this volume.** *Open.*

13. **AND THE FIGURES, AND EVERY ONE OF THEM IS EITHER A COUNT OF DAYS OF THE FLOOD OR A DAY OF A NAMED MONTH WITH ITS OWN FORM, AND NOTHING ELSE.** The store's board, the wind at that wall, the posting, the case on the table, the share with the post, the thirty-nine men under the near gate, the trestle count, the bag on the boards and the line in the ground were all at zero across these ten files. **The keeper of the Redroot gate's day is at zero and was not counted and her year is not named and she is in none of these ten chapters.** The count of this city's silences is 212 at day 311 and 221 at day 320, derived, on no page, in no mouth, **and in none of the ten chapters, and the wall said nothing on all ten days and this batch spends no panel.** The four who have not left are not on a page in any of these ten chapters and were not asked whether it will go and the word *delegation* is at zero. *Open.*

14. **NO LEAD PARAGRAPH OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS CARRIES AN INTERVAL FIGURE.** Every figure that is paid for in these ten days is paid for once, in a mouth or in narration, attached to a decision, and the two instruments the batch uses are a count of days of the flood and a day of a named month with its own form, and **no chapter of this batch uses an ordinal for a month at all.** Fourteen checks were run on the ten files: knock on the stem, player, passage, privilege, unwritten, delegation, System, Earthside, Earthsiders, isekai, Redroot, chapel, Marrow, Alys, Kern, Sundering, Nine Elms, Veyran, Ivenn, choir, First Landing, Steppe, Anchorstone, quest, class, version, silence, this chapter, this volume, in this batch, the reader, *to open nothing*, **and every ordinal for a month and every bare month form — and all thirty-four are at zero on all ten files.** **The word on the second slate is at zero in all ten of these files, which is a decision and not an oversight: the volume's climax is a subtraction of it, and a batch that used it three times in ten days would have spent it. It is on a page in Chapter 0310 and it comes off that page in Chapter 0342.** *Open.*

15. **AND THE HOUSE FIGURES FOR THE TEN DAYS, WITH THE DEFINITIONS BESIDE THEM, ON THE RULE AT §38.4 THAT A FIGURE WITHOUT ITS INSTRUMENT IS NOT PUBLISHED.** **The bolded share of words, measured on the ten files with the heading line included, `---` rules counted and bold marks counted as nothing: 7.7 to 20.8 per cent, a mean of 15.2, in seventy-three spans**, against Volume 06's 6.2 to 21.8 with a mean of 13.8, Volume 01's 10.4 as the model and about 35 as the ceiling. The longest bolded turn in the ten is ninety, in Chapter 0318, and the next are eighty-four in Chapter 0312 and eighty-three in Chapter 0316. **The longest uninterrupted run of speech paragraphs is four, in two of the ten, and three or fewer in the other eight, which is the ceiling in `outline/volume-07.md` §17.6**, and **three runs were over it in the writing pass, in Chapters 0313, 0316 and 0318, and every one of the three was broken with a narration beat and none with a cut speech.** Sentences of forty words or more are **188** across the ten on the definition at `state/batch-summaries/volume-06-batch-0004.md` §8.5.1, and **none is duplicated inside a file and none across the ten.** The word-run check across the ten files, each file's own lead paragraph subtracted, finds a top shared run of **thirteen consecutive words** between Chapters 0312 and 0314, **and it is the chorus frame and not a duplication, on the same reasoning `state/batch-summaries/volume-06-batch-0005.md` §6.2.1 gives for a gatekeeper's lead.** **It stood at sixteen in the writing pass and three pairs sat at fourteen, and every one of those four was three room leads over a market opening the same way, which is the motif `outline/volume-07.md` §14.5 requires, and two of the three leads were rewritten to break, and what is on the page now is the thirteen, which is a different instrument and a cheaper one.** The chorus frame, on the bare head every occurrence of *about four people in*, stands **seventy-three times in 245 paragraphs, one in about three**, against one in about five in Batch 0001 of this volume and one in about four in Volume 06, and **a count is not a target and the figure is published because the volume's cards set none.** The weekday count across the ten files is **forty-two tokens** and every one was read against the day it names and **all forty-two are correct**, and the one fault found was in Chapter 0319, where a Wednesday was written for the day after a Friday and is now a Saturday. *Open.*

16. **THE EXCHANGE, PUBLISHED WITH ITS DEFINITION AND NOT CERTIFIED, because §38.14 records this repository certifying four different values for one measurement because none of them came with a definition.** A *plain speech* is a paragraph carrying a quotation mark and no bold mark; it is *answered* when the very next non-blank paragraph carries a quotation mark and a bold mark. **On the ten files there are twenty-three plain speeches and sixteen of them are answered, sixty-nine point six per cent**, against Volume 06's 316 of 798 at thirty-nine point six. **THE DENOMINATOR IS TWENTY-THREE AND THAT IS THE WHOLE OF THE CAVEAT: these ten chapters are written in narration, the way `outline/volume-07.md` §15 records that this volume is written, and the ratio is not comparable to a volume of two-handers and is published as a measured count with its definition attached and not as a figure to beat.** A later writer who needs this number must publish the counting device first. *Open.*

17. **AND NOTHING IN THESE TEN CHAPTERS IS A POWER.** The gains are a name at the top of a sheet, a sheet with no day and no name on it, nine places walked and eight answers, a folded sheet folded the same way, a date in a word four days old, a door carrying a count and then a word, a form with a notary's name on it and five lines and no number, a space under a last line about the width of two fingers, a woman who says a day out loud at a gate, a boy who says it back, a tin, and a refusal with a condition in it. **None of them is a power. One of them is the price of a power. All of them are heavier than a power.** *Open.*

18. **AND ADRIAN IS NAMED IN FIVE OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS AND IN THE OTHER FIVE HE IS IN NO CHAPTER IN ANY FORM.** He is named in 0311, 0313, 0314, 0317 and 0318, and in every one of the five he is reading a page, **and in every one of the five he is not asked one question about the boundary, and he is aged nowhere in Volume 07, and he is Stage 2 on all fifty days, and no threshold was opened, no working was performed, the Narrow Mark was not opened a third time, the other world is not named once, nobody offered him a workway and he asked for none, and the words *passage* and *privilege* are at zero across these ten files.** In 0312, 0315, 0316, 0319 and 0320 he is in no chapter in any form, **and in 0316 he is in the room on the day the month was named and the chapter does not say so, because a chapter in which people decline to ask him is a chapter he is not in.** *Open.*

---

## 43. Rules established in Volume 07, Chapters 0321–0330 — FOUR NOTICES AND FOUR ROOMS, A NAME READ OFF A PAGE, AND FOUR PEOPLE ON A PAGE

**This section is the third of the five reserved in §33A and it was written by the batch, and by nothing else. §32, §33 and §33A are not changed by it, and §33A is not amended by a batch and no calendar decision is made here. The sixth form is registered at §33A item 2 and it was put on a page in Chapter 0316, and this section states no length for the month, because a batch may not. The volume's one panel is fixed to Chapter 0340 and none of these ten chapters spends it, and the wall says nothing on all ten days.**

**The ten days are days 321 to 330, one chapter to one day, Chapters 0321 to 0330, and the run was recomputed from day 1 being a Tuesday and from nothing else before any prose was written: 321 Sunday, 322 Monday, 323 Tuesday, 324 Wednesday, 325 Thursday, 326 Friday, 327 Saturday, 328 Sunday, 329 Monday, 330 Tuesday. The Bare Month days are the sixth to the fifteenth, the Nth being the day less 315. There is no double day in this batch and there is no double day in this volume, and the chapter number is the day number and there is no offset.**

1. **A MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-TWO WHO DIGS IS ON A PAGE, AND HIS NAME IS READ OFF A PAGE IN A ROOM IN DAYLIGHT WITH A DATE ON THE DOOR, AND NOBODY WENT FOR HIM.** Chapter 0321, day 321, a Sunday, the sixth of that month. **The name is *Nev Cutler*. It is said once, out loud, by the man of about forty-three who keeps nine boxes of other people's papers, off an index in his own hand with four columns including a day, and the index had not been off its shelf in eleven years, and the woman of about twenty-nine who keeps a public register said his name once afterwards and did not put it on anything.** He is fifty-two and it is in his own hand and not a clerk's, and the place the index gives is the flats and not a house, and the trade it gives is digging. **A later writer may give him this name and no other, may not give him a surname he does not have, and may not put his name at the top of a page, because the whole of Chapter 0321 is that a name is worth a person when it can be checked and worth nothing at all when it cannot.** *Open.*

2. **HIS SECOND HANDLE IS A ROD HE DOES NOT CARRY, AND IT IS INVENTED ON THE PAGE IN CHAPTER 0321 AND IN NO OTHER CHAPTER AS AN INTRODUCTION.** It is a length of ash about four feet long with a flat blade on the end of it, ground down on one side and not the other, and it stands on its end in the corner of a hut at the head of the third row against the north wall where the wall is thickest, and it has been there since before the water, and he does not carry it out on any morning. **It is the only one in four hundred miles and no rod has been put in the line in the ground and no rod has been put in anything at all in any chapter of Volume 07.** A rod is a thing a man carries when he is going somewhere to find something, and putting things in holes is his trade and finding things is not his trade, and if he picked it up he would be a different man by the end of the road. *Open, and it is still standing where it was on the tenth of that month and a man stood about nine feet off him in the wind and did not ask him for it.*

3. **THE REASON HE WOULD BE SENT FOR IS ESTABLISHED IN A ROOM, IN DAYLIGHT, WITH A DATE ON THE DOOR, AND NOT IN A YARD, AND NOBODY IS SENT.** Chapter 0321. The room decides that the only instrument in four hundred miles for finding out where a line runs is a person who has spent his life finding out where things are in the ground, and he is eleven miles the other way. The woman of about twenty-nine says the reason nobody has sent out loud in that room with a date on it — **nobody in this city has ever written down a day on which somebody was not sent for, and the register has a column for days, and this is the first thing in nine years she knows the answer to before anybody asks her** — and the man of about fifty-seven did not take the money and the room did not say no. **The line in the ground was not measured, dug, counted, probed or resolved in any of these ten chapters, and nobody went and looked at it, and the two holes nine feet apart were not cleared or measured and no third hole was made and nothing was put in any hole at all.** *Open.*

4. **THE FORM WITH A NOTARY'S NAME ON IT WAS FILLED IN FOR THE FIRST TIME IN FOUR HUNDRED MILES, ON THE SEVENTH DAY OF THAT MONTH, BY THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS THE REGISTER, AND THERE IS NO NUMBER UNDER THE FIRST LINE.** Chapter 0322. It was written out in her own three columns — what was asked, what came back, and the day — because she has never in nine years entered anything in a shape that was not her own, and the first of them said what was asked and the second of them said what came back and the third said the day and the fourth one is not in her book. **What came back was nothing. What came back is always nothing, and that is what a register of nine years is, and she said she had never minded it and minded it that morning.** The space under the last line is still the width of two fingers and nothing has been written in it and that is now on a page. *Open.*

5. **THE ONLY FIGURE THIS CITY CANNOT SUPPORT IS THE ONE THAT WOULD GO UNDER THE FIRST LINE, AND THE ONLY PERSON WHO WILL HAVE TO WRITE IT IS FOUR HUNDRED MILES AWAY WITH A COPY IN A COAT.** Chapter 0322. The man of about forty-one in a very good coat, with a hand inside a coat with nothing in it, came back on a day of his own choosing having said he would come back when the room had put something on the form and not said when, and said the finding out loud: **a day is not a date, and a day in a word with no figure under the name has to have a number written in it by whoever is holding the copy, and whatever he writes will be wrong, and it will be his handwriting, and he will not be able to tell anybody how he got it.** That is `bible/power-system.md` §42 item 8 and this chapter is its price, said by the person paying it. Hettis Roke is in the same room and will not put a number under a line and gives a reason that is his own and is not the reason he gave on the fifty-ninth, and **he turned the case face up and did not open it.** *Open.*

6. **A REGISTER WITH NINE YEARS IN IT HAS FOUR INSTRUMENTS IN IT AND NO COLUMN SAYING WHICH ONE ANY ROW IS IN, AND A PERSON READING THE BOOK TODAY CANNOT TELL.** Chapter 0323, day 323, a Tuesday, the eighth. Found by the woman of about twenty-nine in daylight with a date on the door, out loud, and she said she wanted it said out loud before she said anything else. A day counted from the water; a day counted from the water with a different word in front of it, running on past the number the first instrument stops at without a mark; a day with a name in front of it, of which there are about nine hundred and two different names for the same stretch of time; **and a day with a name in front of it that is three weeks old and has no figure under the name at all.** She would not put a fourth column in and she would not refuse one, and she said the worse thing in the same book out loud and said the room was not going to get to it. *Open, and **Chapter 0314's finding — no column for the day a sheet went out — is not resolved in this chapter and is not resolved anywhere in it.***

7. **THE ROWS AT THE BACK OF THAT REGISTER ARE NINE YEARS OLD, ARE IN AN INSTRUMENT THIS CITY STOPPED WRITING IN YEARS AGO, AND ARE THE ONLY ROWS IN THE BOOK THAT HAVE A DAY IN THEM THAT A PERSON COULD STAND BEHIND.** Chapter 0323, said out loud by a woman of about thirty-three who has been right about that room twice without being told: **she can read the handwriting and she can read every word of what was asked, and she cannot tell you what day any of it happened on, and neither can anybody else in four hundred miles.** A register with three columns and no column for the day a sheet went out cannot tell a question that took four days from one that took nine, and nine rows at the back of it cannot tell anybody the day at all, **and those are the same fault nine years apart and the woman keeping the book found both of them on the same morning.** The one column in that book that works without a date in it is the column for what came back. *Open.*

8. **THE NINETEEN STONES AND THE EIGHT THAT ARE DIFFERENT WERE CARRIED TO A PLACE IN CHAPTER 0323 AND WERE NOT RESOLVED.** The man of about forty-three read the sheet out a second time, which took about four minutes and which nobody stopped, and said what he wanted said: **there are four instruments in a book in this room and a clerk's hand a hundred and forty years old on a table in this room, and they have got nothing to do with each other, because they are five instruments and none of the five has ever had to be read by anybody who did not already know which one they were holding.** The eight names at the foot of the copy were not read and no chapter of this batch read them. *Open.*

9. **THE MIDPOINT. ADRIAN'S PLAN IS CORRECT ABOUT THE FACTS AND WORSE ABOUT THE COST, AND TAMSIN QUILL'S DECISION IS THAT NOBODY GOES, AND IT IS IN ABOUT SIX SENTENCES AND THE SIXTH IS THE ONE THAT COSTS.** Chapters 0324 and 0325, days 324 and 325, a Wednesday and a Thursday, the ninth and the tenth. The plan is in a room in nine words: the line cannot be settled here, the only page in four hundred miles that says where it runs is four hundred miles inland, and somebody has to go and get it, and there are four people in this city who came here to fetch pages and **he is not asking any of them whether they will go.** The decision is: a man who has paid for half a holding is not going to be answered by four hundred miles and a winter; the people who are going to pay for the not-answering are nine households on a lane; a road that a man walks once in a good season is a road those nine households did not give back on the thirty-ninth day of the Short Month; the line is settled here by what is here; what is here is two accounts that disagree and a line nobody has measured; **and the settlement is that it is not settled, and that is a thing that can be written down with a day on it, and a thing that cannot be written down cannot be argued about, and it has been argued about for forty years.** *Open.*

10. **HE DOES NOT ARGUE IT AND HE WORKS IT OUT IN A YARD ABOUT ELEVEN MILES THE OTHER WAY, STANDING ABOUT NINE FEET OFF THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-TWO WHO DIGS, WHO TELLS HIM NOTHING AND HAS NOT BEEN ASKED ANYTHING, AND THE CHAPTER SAYS HE WORKED IT OUT AND DOES NOT SAY HE WAS RIGHT.** Chapter 0325, at about the fourth hour of the afternoon, on the flats, in the wind, for the rest of the afternoon. **The nine feet is the distance at which a man working does not have to stop. Two men who have never spoken can stand in the same wind for the length of about nine spadefuls without either of them owing the other a sentence, and the not-asking was not arranged by the man who came out and he is the only man in four hundred miles who could have arranged it.** *Open.*

11. **THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN LOST A SEASON AND STILL HAS A CERTAINTY, AND HE NAMED BOTH OUT LOUD AND ASKED FOR BOTH TO BE ENTERED, AND NOBODY IN ANY CHAPTER OF THIS VOLUME TELLS HIM HE IS WRONG BECAUSE NOBODY IN THIS VOLUME IS WRONG.** He stood up with a length of new rope over his arm and said it in Chapter 0324, and he asked the only question anybody expected from him in Chapter 0325 — *is there a day on them* — and he was told: **there is a day on the room and there is nothing at the top of any of them, and that is four days, and if it were three days it would not be a notice, it would be a delivery.** He also said in Chapter 0325: **nobody is going to tell me it is the right thing to do**, and was told that nobody was going to tell him the other either, and asked for that to be entered in a room and not in a book. *Open.*

12. **FOUR NOTICES WENT OUT IN FOUR DAYS AND ALL FOUR ARE FOUR DAYS, EACH ONE NAMES ITS OWN MORNING INSIDE ITS OWN CHAPTER, ALL FOUR ARE UNSIGNED WITH NOTHING AT THE TOP OF ANY OF THEM, AND ALL FOUR GO TO A PLACE AND NOT TO A HOUSE.** `bible/power-system.md` §33A item 7 is the register and this is the page. The first went out on the eleventh of that month at about the second hour of the afternoon for a room on the fifteenth, in a hand that is not the hand of anybody in that room, and the place on it is eight houses in a row with a gap for a cart between two of them, **out past the last named house, which is the ninth of the nine places on a sheet of road, and a woman walked nine places in three days and got no answer at that one and wrote nothing on the ninth line.** The second went out on the twelfth at about the seventh hour for a room on the sixteenth and reached nine places. The third went out on the thirteenth in the morning for a room on the seventeenth. The fourth went out on the fourteenth at about the ninth hour for a room on the eighteenth. **Every one of them is a question in the words of the person it is about, written by that person, and the person whose words they are is in the room on the day the room is and not on the day the sheet goes out.** *Open, and a later chapter may not reduce any of these four to three days and may not put a name at the top of any of them and may not print the string.*

13. **THE PRICE OF NO NAME AT THE TOP OF A SHEET IS THAT NOBODY IN THIS CITY CAN ASK WHETHER IT ARRIVED AND NOBODY CAN SAY IT DID NOT, AND THAT WAS SAID OUT LOUD IN A ROOM ON THE ELEVENTH OF THAT MONTH.** Chapter 0326. A hand is not a page, a hand stays in the hand that made it until that person dies, and **a sheet with a person's hand on it cannot be argued with at all, and that is not a good thing, and it is the only reason the instrument works** — said by the man of about thirty-one who carries things for a living, who said he had been saying it for nine years and that nobody had ever wanted to hear it. **AND THE SECOND OF THE FOUR IS COPIED NINE TIMES AND PUT IN A BAG IN ONE COAT, AND A COPY DOES NOT WEAKEN THE CLAIM, AND THE CHAPTER THAT MULTIPLIES IT IS THE ONE THAT GIVES THE REASON, WHICH IS STANDING AND NOT A HAND: nine copies are one hand nine times over and not nine hands, and there is nobody in four hundred miles with standing to argue with a notice, because the only person who could say what one means is the person whose hand it is and that person is not going to be asked and has not been asked, and a person who wants to be arguing with a sheet has to put a name at the top of it, and a name is the only thing on that sheet which would change what it is. Chapter 0327, and the room did not say the reason out loud.** **And a place with eight houses in it is a place and a house at the end of a lane past two gates is only a house, and you cannot leave anything at a house without a name, and that is the second time in about a month that a room over a market has come to the difference and the first time was a woman of about fifty at a shut gate deciding on her own that a sheet on a wall was about nobody, and she has not been told any of this and is not going to be.** *Open.*

14. **THE SECOND OF THE FOUR IS A MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-FIVE, WHO IS ON A PAGE ALREADY — HE IS IN CHAPTER 0309 — SO HE IS A RETURN AND NOT AN INTRODUCTION, AND A STATE FILE THAT PUBLISHES A ZERO FOR HIM IS WRONG TWICE.** He is in Chapter 0327 and in Chapter 0331 and in no other chapter of this batch, and **his second handle is invented on the page in Chapter 0327: his left boot, mended at the front with a length of cord that is not new.** He said the words *I am not going to be asked* out loud in that room, and nobody asked him, and **nobody has been asked in any of these ten chapters whether any of the four of them is going to go, and no chapter of this batch asks any of them, and no chapter of this batch sends anybody to ask them.** *Open.*

15. **THE THIRD OF THE FOUR IS A MAN OF ABOUT FORTY-EIGHT AND IS ON A PAGE FOR THE FIRST TIME IN CHAPTER 0328, DAY 328, A SUNDAY, THE THIRTEENTH, AND HE IS A PERSON AND NOT A FORM.** A folding knife with a hinge that has gone stiff and does not spring, which he opens and shuts twice with the thumb of the other hand to make the joint take, and then it will hold about nine minutes, and **he is a man who goes up a ladder for a living when he can get the work and there is not enough of it, and he said that in a room with a date on the door rather than say it at a door.** He also mended the catch on a window in that room that had not shut properly for nine years, in about four minutes, with a knife and his thumbnail, without being asked and without asking anybody for a tool, and **the room he did it in is a room over a market and the draught in it was the reason nobody in that room had said anything about the cold for a fortnight, and a window which has been letting a hand's width of weather in for nine years is not the same thing as a window that has been letting a hand's width of a person in, and the man who closed it is not the woman of about fifty at a shut gate on another road and has never met her and is not going to.** The woman of about twenty-nine asked him the first question anybody has asked him in this flood and it was about his trade and not about the boundary and not about the sheet and not about the four days, and she said she would not ask a second one. *Open.*

16. **THE FOURTH OF THE FOUR IS A WOMAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-ONE AND SHE SAYS THE NO HERSELF, UNASKED, IN FOUR WORDS, IN A ROOM WITH A DATE ON THE DOOR, ON THE FOURTEENTH OF THAT MONTH, A MONDAY.** Chapter 0329. Her second handle is invented on the page in that chapter: **a stick of hazel she carries in her hand and does not lean on.** Nobody asked her and she said it before the question could be finished, and **the room did not argue with it, and nobody in that room is going to be told she is wrong and nobody in that room is going to be told she is right.** She also put two things on a page in the same room in her own words, and the second of them is the whole of the instruction the four of them have and is in other words and not the string: **there are four of them, there is no word in this city for what the four of them are, and she has looked for one for a long time and there is not one and that is not an injustice, that is just the language; and we have never once opened anything, and that was the whole of the instruction and it was given in about four words by a man who is not in this room.** Nobody wrote either of them down. *Open.*

17. **THE FIRST OF THE FOUR IS A WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-ONE AND IS ON A PAGE FOR THE FIRST TIME IN CHAPTER 0330, DAY 330, A TUESDAY, THE FIFTEENTH, IN A ROOM THAT IS NOT THE ROOM OVER A MARKET, IN DAYLIGHT, WITH THE DAY IN CHALK ON ITS DOOR, IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINE PEOPLE.** Her second handle is invented on the page in that chapter: **a pen with the nib ground narrow, cut down with a knife, because the one she was given was too broad and she is not going to be given another one.** The room is a room over a shop on the low ground, and the date on its door was put there at about the sixth hour that morning by a woman of about thirty-three who keeps the room and has a bread board on the sill of the window in it, **and nobody in four hundred miles had put a date on that door before, and there is no column of them going up the frame and nothing rubbed out, and a door with one date on it and nothing above it is not a record, it is a notice.** Tamsin Quill is in that room and the woman of about twenty-nine who keeps a public register is not in it and is not in any room with her. **She said yes in her own words to a question nobody asked her, and nobody in that room asked her whether it would go, and she does not know what she is for, and she put her own handwriting flat on the table and read it for about as long as it takes to fill a pail and had the cut-down pen in her hand and did not write anything with it, because there is nothing on that table for her to write on.** *Open.*

18. **THE GIRL OF SEVENTEEN IS IN CHAPTER 0327 AND IS ON NO ROLL OF ANYTHING AND THE NOTICE REACHED NINE PLACES AND NOT HER, AND THE CHAPTER DOES NOT SOLVE IT.** She copied the second of the four onto a plain slate, both sides, the way she has copied every asking anybody has written in this flood, and she has signed none of them and she was not going to sign this one, and **she asked the only question she asked in that room, which was where does this one go, and was told in front of about nine people that she is not one of the nine and that the person who answered does not know the answer to the second half of it.** She then went out of the room about a quarter of an hour after the carrier and went the other way, down to the low ground, and **she is on no list and she was never on one, and being unfindable is not a qualification and no chapter of this volume resolves her.** *Open, and her second use in this volume is the copy and the question and not a reading out.*

19. **AND THE THING THAT IS ABOUT FOUR PEOPLE AND NOT ABOUT A FORM, WHICH A CHAPTER MAY NOT GIVE A WORD.** Four sheets went out in four days and four people are on a page in four chapters, and **the word that is at zero across three hundred and thirty chapters stays at zero, and a chapter may say four people and may not give them a word for what they are, and the woman of about sixty-one said so out loud in a room on the fourteenth of that month and it went onto nothing.** Three of the four have said yes in their own words by the fifteenth and one has said no in a sentence that is hers, and **a chapter may not print the string and a later chapter may put the fact on a page in other words.** *Open.*

20. **AND NOTHING WAS MEASURED, DUG, COUNTED, PROBED, RESOLVED OR CONNECTED, AND THE WORD IS AT ZERO IN ALL TEN FILES.** Nobody went and looked at the line in the ground in any of these ten chapters, nobody put a rod in it or in anything, the two holes nine feet apart were not cleared or measured, and **the word on the register-keeper's second slate is at zero in all ten of these files, which is a decision and not an oversight: it comes off that slate in chalk with a cloth in Chapter 0342 and after that it is on no page in the world.** The one clause about that mark that may be put on a page once is on the page once and is not repeated in any of these ten chapters, and **none of the three sentences this manuscript has used about that word is in any mouth in any of these ten chapters and none was improved on.** The woman of about fifty at the shut gate is in none of these ten chapters, was asked nothing, and nobody was sent to her. The keeper of the Redroot gate is in none of them, her days were not counted and her year is not named. The ninth line was not moved, dated, read aloud, taken down, thanked for or resolved, and the man who wrote it is in none of these ten chapters. The board above the door of the store is not written on and the wall above it is not washed. The case is shut, the schedule is not filled in and is not the subject of a chapter, and the barrow of boards at the shut gate and the barrow on the flats were not joined. *All open.*

21. **AND THE FIGURES, AND NONE OF THEM IS AN INTERVAL FIGURE AND NONE OF THEM IS IN A LEAD.** The only two instruments these ten days use are **a count of days of the flood** and **a day of the Bare Month with its own form**, and no chapter of this batch uses an ordinal for a month or any bare month form. **No lead paragraph of these ten chapters carries an interval figure at all.** Every elapsed count a later writer wants is a subtraction from `bible/power-system.md` §33A item 5 and is not printed in any of these ten chapters. **The count of this city's silences is 222 at day 321 and 231 at day 330, derived, on no page, in no mouth, and in none of the ten chapters, and the wall said nothing on all ten days and this batch spends no panel.** The four who have not left are on a page in four of these ten chapters and their days are not counted. *Open.*

22. **AND THE HOUSE FIGURES FOR THE TEN DAYS, WITH THE DEFINITIONS BESIDE THEM, ON THE RULE AT §38.4 THAT A FIGURE WITHOUT ITS INSTRUMENT IS NOT PUBLISHED.** The bolded share of words, measured on the ten files with the heading line included, `---` rules counted and bold marks counted as nothing: **7.0 to 18.4 per cent, a mean of 13.4, in fifty spans**, against Volume 06's 6.2 to 21.8 with a mean of 13.8, Volume 01's 10.4 as the model and about 35 as the ceiling. **The longest bolded turn in the ten is ninety, in Chapter 0329, and the next are eighty-eight in 0324 and eighty-five in 0321 and 0326.** The longest uninterrupted run of speech paragraphs is **four, the ceiling in `outline/volume-07.md` §17.6, in three of the ten, and three or fewer in the other seven, and no run in the writing pass was over the ceiling.** Sentences of forty words or more are **230** across the ten on the definition at `state/batch-summaries/volume-06-batch-0004.md` §8.5.1, and **none is duplicated inside a file and none across the ten.** Twenty-one weekday tokens stand across the ten files and **every one was read in its sentence against the day it names and all twenty-one are correct**, and every one of them is either the day the chapter is or a named day of the week pointing at a chapter that is that day. *Open.*

23. **AND THE FIGURE IN THE PARAGRAPH ABOVE THAT IS THE ONE THIS BATCH GOT WORSE THAN THE TWO BEFORE IT, PUBLISHED BECAUSE A COUNT IS NOT A TARGET AND A FIGURE NOBODY HAS READ IS NOT A MEASUREMENT.** **The chorus frame, on the bare head every occurrence of *about four people in*, stands one hundred and forty-five times in two hundred and eighty-nine paragraphs on the ten files, one in two, against one in about three in Batch 0002, one in about five in Batch 0001 and one in about four across Volume 06. The review repair pass added one to both figures and took none out, because the one clause it added to Chapter 0327 is a chorus clause, and the figure a later writer compares against is one hundred and forty-five and not one hundred and forty-four.** Two files, Chapters 0327 and 0328, run at one in three and two, and the two lowest bolded shares in the batch are the same two files. **The cause is named and it is a fault and not a virtue: two of the ten chapters are rooms in which somebody is not being asked anything, and a chapter in which nobody is asked anything is a chapter in which the narrator's one counting habit has nothing else to attach itself to.** A repair pass has the instrument and the method: on a word-bounded search for the frame followed by a clause beginning with a determiner, a pronoun or a numeral, the frame can be deleted and the clause chain re-coordinated, and the correct action is to take about forty of them out of the two files that are over the line rather than to thin a chapter in which the frame is doing a job. *Open and routed. `outline/volume-07.md` §17.10 is where the frame is described and this paragraph does not change it.*

24. **THE WORD-RUN CHECK ACROSS THE TEN FILES, EACH FILE'S OWN LEAD PARAGRAPH SUBTRACTED, FINDS A TOP SHARED RUN OF TWENTY-FOUR CONSECUTIVE WORDS, BETWEEN CHAPTERS 0321 AND 0324, AND IT IS THE ROOM-LEAD FRAME.** The next three are twenty-two, and **all four of the four are motifs and not duplications: the room lead that says the light, the cold and what is under about four pairs of hands, and the chorus frame with the man of about fifty-seven's descriptor in it** — on the same reasoning `state/batch-summaries/volume-06-batch-0005.md` §6.2.1 gives for a gatekeeper's lead and `outline/volume-07.md` §20.5 gives for the physical-work lead. Against eighty-seven between Chapters 0299 and 0300 in Volume 06 and twenty-four in Batch 0001, **which is the same figure and the same class of thing.** The loose reading, in which each word only has to appear somewhere in the other file, is not an instrument and was not run. *Open.*

25. **THE EXCHANGE, PUBLISHED WITH ITS DEFINITION AND NOT CERTIFIED.** A *plain speech* is a paragraph carrying a quotation mark and no bold mark; it is *answered* when the very next non-blank paragraph carries a quotation mark and a bold mark. **On the ten files there are forty-eight plain speeches and twenty-four of them are answered, fifty per cent**, against Volume 06's 316 of 798 at thirty-nine point six and Batch 0002's 23 of 24. **THE DENOMINATOR IS FORTY-EIGHT AND THAT IS THE WHOLE OF THE CAVEAT**, and it is larger than Batch 0002's twenty-three, which is the one figure in this item that moved in the right direction. These ten chapters are written in narration, the way `outline/volume-07.md` §15 records that this volume is written, and the ratio is not comparable to a volume of two-handers. A later writer who needs this number must publish the counting device first. *Open.*

26. **AND THE BOUNDED-STRING LIST, RUN ON THE TEN FILES AND NOT INSIDE ONE CHAPTER.** At zero: *knock* on the stem, *player*, *passage*, *privilege*, *unwritten*, *delegation*, *System*, *Earthside*, *Earthsiders*, *isekai*, *Redroot*, *chapel*, *Marrow*, *Alys*, *Kern*, *Sundering*, *Nine Elms*, *Veyran*, *Ivenn*, *choir*, *quest* on a word boundary, *class*, *version*, *silence*, the word on the second slate, *this chapter*, *this volume*, *in this batch*, *the reader*, **the string *to open nothing***, and every ordinal for a month and every bare month form. **Two false positives were checked and neither is a fault: *quest* inside *question* in all ten files, which the word boundary excludes, and *Steppe* inside *stepped* in Chapter 0301, which is a file of Batch 0001's and not this batch's.** A stray closing quotation mark is at zero on all ten files, **which is a claim about these ten files and not a volume, and the nineteen on a page in Chapters 0281 to 0290 are still unrepaired and still belong to a Volume 07 review-repair pass and to no batch.** The forty-word near-duplicate sentence across the 0254 and 0262 boundary is still unrepaired and is still not a batch's to see. *Open.*

27. **AND THE DESCRIPTOR MAP, AT SENTENCE LEVEL, WORD-BOUNDED, ON THE TEN FILES, WITH THE CO-PRESENCE CHECK ON THE SAME BOUNDED DEFINITION.** *Man of about forty-three* 8, *man of about fifty-two* 7, *woman of about twenty-nine* 7, *man of about fifty-seven* 7, *woman of about thirty-three* 5, *man of about thirty-one* 5, *woman of about sixty-one* 4, *woman of about twenty-one* 4, *woman of about fifty* 2, *person of about twenty-one* 2, *man of about twenty-five* 1, *woman of about twenty-eight* 1, *man of about forty-eight* 1. **The four crowded descriptors of Volume 06 — *woman of about twenty-four*, *man of about thirty-six*, *man of about thirty-four* and *woman of about thirty* — are at zero on all ten of these files, and the man of about thirty-six who holds gates on a turn is in none of them and is carried forward to Chapters 0338 and 0345 on the two instructions against one that `outline/volume-07.md` §6.4 and §14.3 give.** The co-presence prohibition on a paragraph carrying the man of about thirty-four and a woman of about thirty-four together holds at zero and is not a finding about anything, because neither descriptor is in the batch. **The unbounded search returns eighteen hits for *man of about fifty* on these ten files and every one of the eighteen is the string inside *woman of about fifty*, which is the shut-gate keeper, and the correct action on a hit there is to go and read the page.** *Open.*

28. **THREE DESCRIPTORS THIS BATCH ADDED ARE ON NO PAGE AND CARRY A ZERO, AND A FOURTH IS A RETURN AND NOT AN INTRODUCTION.** A woman of about twenty-one, a man of about forty-eight and a woman of about sixty-one were each measured at zero across all fifty files of Volume 06 before they were taken, and a man of about twenty-five was on a page in Chapter 0309 by a person in a room in daylight with a date on the door. **A writer who calls him new is wrong, and a state file that publishes a zero for him is wrong twice.** *Open.*

29. **AND ADRIAN IS NAMED IN TWO OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS, AND IN THE OTHER EIGHT HIS NAME IS NOT ON THE PAGE, AND THOSE EIGHT ARE NOT ONE THING.** He is named in Chapter 0321 and in Chapter 0325. **AND THE COUNT OF TWO IS RIGHT AND THE CHAPTERS ARE 0321 AND 0325 AND NOT 0321 AND 0324, and a state file that says 0324 is wrong on the page and a later writer who trusts it will go and look for a name in a chapter that has none.** In 0321 he is in a room reading a clerk's hand off a sheet pinned to the corner of a deed and puts a finger a finger's width above the last line without touching it, and in 0325 he is on eleven miles of flats about nine feet off a man with a spade saying nothing, which is the ninth time in this volume that the not-asking is shown rather than asserted.

**THE EIGHT SPLIT INTO TWO, AND SAYING A NAME AND PRINTING ONE ARE TWO DIFFERENT ACTS ON A PAGE, AND A WRITER WHO MERGES THEM WILL COUNT THREE CHAPTERS INSTEAD OF TWO.** In seven of them — 0322, 0323, 0326, 0327, 0328, 0329 and 0330 — he is in no chapter in any form. In the eighth, 0324, he is in the room for the whole morning reading a clerk's hand and turning the sheet over to look at the back of it, **and the name is not on that page, because Tamsin Quill says his name once and the chapter does not print it, so he is *he* and the man who brought the plan from the first word to the last and a reader cannot put a name to him.** In 0325 his name is on the page twice and a man out of doors in the wind is not the case the naming rule was written for, **so the rule the outline gives and the page disagree once in every ten and the page is what a reader can check.**

**He is aged nowhere in Volume 07 and is Stage 2 on all fifty days, and no threshold was opened, no working was performed, the Narrow Mark was not opened a third time, the other world is not named once across the ten files, nobody offered him a workway and he asked for none, and the words *passage* and *privilege* are at zero.** *Open.*

30. **AND NOTHING IN THESE TEN CHAPTERS IS A POWER.** The gains are a man's name read off a page in four seconds, a rod that stands against a wall, a day on which nobody was sent for, a register filled in for the first time, a number under a first line that nobody can supply, four ways of writing a day and no column for which one, nine rows at the back of a book that a person cannot date, six sentences and a decision that nobody goes, a man standing about nine feet off another man in the wind for an afternoon, four sheets in four hands with nothing at the top of any of them, a window in a room over a market that had not shut for nine years, a stick of hazel a woman carries and does not lean on, four words nobody asked for, a pen with the nib ground narrow, and a door on the low ground with one date on it and nothing above it. **None of them is a power. One of them is the price of a power. All of them are heavier than a power.** *Open.*

---

## 44. Rules established in Volume 07, Chapters 0331–0340 — THREE ROOMS AND TWO THAT WERE NOT ASKED, THE FIFTH NOTICE, A DIGGER'S CONDITION, AND THE VOLUME'S ONE PANEL

**This section is the fourth of the five reserved in §33A and it was written by the batch, and by nothing else. §32, §33 and §33A are not changed by it, and §33A is not amended by a batch and no calendar decision is made here. The sixth form is registered at §33A item 2 and it was put on a page in Chapter 0316, and this section states no length for the month, because a batch may not. §33A item 4 is the day map and §33A item 5 is the anchor table, and both are read and neither is added to. §33A item 7 carries the six notices of Volume 07 and this batch wrote the fifth of them and the fifth is on the page here.**

1. **THE DAY MAP REPRODUCES EXACTLY, AND IT WAS RECOMPUTED FROM DAY 1 BEING A TUESDAY AND FROM NOTHING ELSE BEFORE ANY PROSE WAS WRITTEN.** The prompt's table was then checked rather than believed, it reproduces, and it was thrown away.
